| **.136f (R207)** | **FIELD FIX - 'web ui ist wieder erreichbar ... aber jetzt kommt wieder 403' + a loaded gun defused.** The .136e field log: .136e confirmed installed (build stamp + single state pushes - the .136d twin watcher is GONE), host healthy (HOST_CHECK verdict OK) - but command POSTs 403 again. Root-cause analysis against Moonraker SOURCE (authorization.py, read this time instead of guessed): 403 = 'Unapproved Origin' from check_cors, 401 = untrusted IP, and the Host header is irrelevant for auth. Two ways my R206 fix could still fail in the field, both fixed: (1) A WRITTEN SITE FILE IS NOT A LIVE CONFIG - when the nginx reload silently fails, nginx keeps serving the OLD site (page loads, every command 403). install_web.sh now SELF-TESTS the live guard after every reload: a foreign-origin POST must be rejected BY NGINX (403, moonraker does not even need to be up), a same-origin POST must not be 403; on failure one reload retry (nginx -s reload), then the exact one-line fix is printed. The sandbox E2E exercised the retry path for real (first reload failed, retry passed). (2) CASE TRAP: the browser lowercases the Origin header but sends the Host exactly as typed - a bookmarked 'http://Ender-3.local:7126/' failed the case-sensitive origin==host map and answered 403. The map is case-insensitive (~*) now, and the proxied Host header is back to $host without port (field-proven original; auth never reads it). E2E with real nginx 1.26: full matrix green INCLUDING the mixed-case trap (200, was 403 with the old map), foreign origin 403 at the proxy, moonraker never sees an Origin. (3) RIDING ALONG - THE LOADED GUN: the duplicate-watcher matcher matched 'r2_postproc' ANYWHERE in a command line - a grep/nano/pgrep/bash that merely MENTIONS the name looked like a twin and the runtime sweep would have KILLED it (the new T6e test killed its own bash wrapper during development - in the field your 'grep r2_postproc ...' would have died). Now EXECUTION precision: a python interpreter with an r2_postproc*.py file among its ARGUMENTS. Plus: the sweep names the rival's full command line (from /proc) so the next doubled-push log says WHAT the second pusher was; update.sh runs a SECOND read-only watcher census at the very end (klippy up, after the boot races - the .136d doubling happened right AFTER a clean early census); the r142 bracket lint reads ast string-joins now (the .136d ROOT respond hid 'pkill -f ' + 'r2_postproc.py' across two lines - found and bracketed). Field-bug class #14: a written configuration is not the active configuration - verify behavior, not files. Battery: r197 430/0 (+T6d execution precision, +T6e rival-path sweep), r142 132/0 (ast lint + 2 fixtures), r81 23/23 (stamp-only re-freeze, proven), macro_render 1910/1910, r110 10/10, tier report 22/22. |
| **.136e (R206)** | **FIELD FIX - 'web ui sagt jetzt 403 forbidden': Moonraker's origin check killed every command - and the .136c fix had never reached the Pi.** Two stacked causes, both found in code and fixed. (1) THE 403: Moonraker answers 403 to every request whose Origin header it does not know - and browsers send Origin on EVERY command POST (same-origin GETs carry none). That is exactly why the web-LCD showed live values but 'kann da nix weiter machen' since .136b: GET /server/r2_menu/live passed (no Origin), every button's POST /printer/gcode/script died with 403 (the page shows 'Moonraker lehnte ab (403)'). FIX: the nginx site strips the Origin header at the proxy (the page is same-origin by construction - the header carries no information Moonraker could use) AND nginx takes over the browser-CSRF check itself: a map at the top of the site file lets only requests pass whose Origin is empty (GETs, curl, tools) or exactly this site (http://<host> == Host header) - a foreign website's Origin is answered 403 BEFORE it reaches the printer, at least as strict as the check it replaces. (2) THE DEPLOYMENT GAP: update.sh never synced web/r2_menu.py into moonraker's components/ - the .136b installer ran once, so the R204 slim query NEVER REACHED THE FIELD (the 403 report made that visible). install_web.sh gains --update-only: update.sh refreshes component + nginx site on EVERY package update now (idempotent, nginx -t gated, clean NO-OP when no web-LCD is installed - the web-LCD stays opt-in). Riding along: the installer writes the nginx site via sudo when /etc/nginx is not user-writable (site chmod 644, read fallbacks for status/port detection - both found by the live E2E), the proxied Host header keeps its port ($http_host), and --status reports whether the CSRF guard is present. VERIFIED END TO END with real nginx 1.26 + a fake moonraker: page 200, live GET proxied without Origin, the same-origin command POST proxied WITHOUT Origin (the 403 case - now 200), a foreign-origin POST answered 403 BY NGINX (never reaches moonraker - proven by the hit log), --update-only restores a stale component and keeps the port. Field-bug class #13: the auth layer between proxy and API (+ code in the zip is not code on the Pi - deploy what you ship). Battery: r197 428/0 (+4 web-LCD tests), r142 130/0, r81 23/23 (stamp-only re-freeze, proven), macro_render 1910/1910, r110 10/10, tier report 22/22. |
| **.136d (R205)** | **FIELD FEEDBACK - 'sudo zeigt uns die Wahrheit' - a measurement artifact, now impossible to misread.** The .136c field log: update ran clean (build R2-2026-10-29.136c confirmed, daemon on new code) - then the user checked the watcher by hand: the check showed ONE process without sudo and THREE lines with sudo - read as hidden watchers that only root can see. TRUTH: the two extra lines WERE the 'sudo pgrep' command itself. pgrep -f matches the FULL command line; pgrep excludes only its own PID, not the sudo wrapper pair that carries the same pattern in its command line; /proc is world-readable, so plain pgrep hides nothing here. Real watcher count: exactly 1 (PID 2125) - and a second .136+ watcher cannot even start (kernel flock, R200 - it refuses loudly). MY bug: the daemon's flock-refusal hint and the .101-era verify line both told the user to run the bare pattern - the documentation manufactured this trap. Fixes: (1) EVERY shipped pgrep/pkill on the watcher uses the [r] bracket form now ('pgrep -af [r]2_postproc.py' - the regex cannot match the checker's own command line, with or without sudo); (2) update.sh's WATCHER CENSUS now SAYS the number + PID after every update ('[ok] watcher census: exactly 1 r2-postproc watcher (PID 2125) - started with this update, running the new code') instead of a silent ok - silence is what sent the user to pgrep; census >1 prints ONE fix command (sudo bash update.sh), census 0 keeps the start hint; (3) the root pkill in update.sh is bracket-protected too - an unbracketed root pkill matches its OWN sudo wrapper and could signal its parent mid-run; same fix in the daemon's root-relic takeover hint; (4) r197 T6a asserts the bracket form in the refusal hint; (5) NEW r142 lint: no unbracketed pgrep/pkill watcher pattern may ship on any live surface (scripts, daemon, manual, test docs - the README changelog is exempt, history may quote old commands). MANUAL troubleshooting row added, DISPLAYS_WEB_UI.pdf field-bug classes completed to (12) ((10) watcher boot doubling + silent klippy-down, (11) live-endpoint load). Field-bug class #12: measurement artifacts - the diagnostic shows up in its own measurement. Battery: r197 424/0, r142 130/0 (new lint), r81 23/23 (stamp-only re-freeze, PROVEN: the new module run with the old stamp string reproduces all 23 .136c goldens byte-identical), r110 all 10 scenarios, macro_render 1910/1910, tier report 22/22. |

(3) THE TEXTURE TOOL NEVER BUILT A FILE (macro level, R189): jinja's default() only fires on UNDEFINED, so an EMPTY _PREVIEW.name (push skipped while busy / not arrived yet) never fell through to preview_file - and the preview push carries the BARE basename, so files from subfolders were 'not found'. preview_file (set synchronously at selection, WITH its subfolder path) is the primary source now, _PREVIEW.name only a fallback; SCALE already read preview_file directly (chain audited: macro -> watcher -> apply_scale -> auto-select, no bug found - the 'does not print' symptom is the no-autostart UX path, covered by the new feedback). (5) ONE unified R2-D2 melody set project-wide, silent while printing: r2_start = work begins (every create/fix press), r2_gen = queued-job build, r2_done = success, r2_confirm = quick confirmations; quiet_print now also covers start/gen/done - during a print the LED carries the feedback. | **.125 (R166)** | **Boot-blocker hotfix (.124 halted at connect) + the printing LED breathes again + stale-daemon proof.** (1) **HOTFIX:** .124 died at startup with `Internal error during connect ... gcode_macro GEN_BRICKKIT ... Unexpected end of template, looking for 'endif'` - the new OUT= dispatch lines in GEN_BRICKKIT/GEN_FIGURES sat at COLUMN 0 inside the gcode body. Klipper does not read bodies with our regexes: configfile.py strips every line at the first `#`, then `configparser.RawConfigParser(strict=False, inline_comment_prefixes=(';', '#'))` parses the file - and an UNINDENTED line inside a value is a NEW OPTION (`name=value`), so the `gcode:` value was silently truncated inside the open `{% if %}` block. Fixed (indentation); GEN_FIGURES carried the same landmine one macro later. New **tools/r166_template_check.py** replays klipper's EXACT pipeline over every macro template in the repo: per-line #-strip, the configparser call, `jinja2.Environment('{%', '%}', '{', '}')` compile of every gcode value, an option allowlist per section type (a column-0 line shows up as a bogus option), and a diff between what our regexes see and what configparser returns. It reproduces the .124 error word for word on the broken file and passes on the fixed one (21 cfg files, 2062 sections, 337 templates) - the whole battery stayed green on .124 because the render test extracts bodies with regexes that tolerate column-0 lines; this class of bug can never ship again. (2) **Printing LED motion back (user request):** the .123 static blue was the emergency fix; the real budget analysis says a slow 4 s breathing at 5 fps costs 5 serial msg/s = ~1% of the 450 msg/s budget - the .122 death queue (75/100 LED messages) came from 24 fps plus readback resends; a 3 s host stall now queues 15 of 100 messages instead of 72. r164's LED guard enforces `frame_rate <= 5` + static/breathing layer for r2_printing. (3) **Stale-daemon proof (the lithophane V2 mystery):** the litho FLAT code in .122+ is proven correct - a full repro on the user's Bun2.jpg with the exact field defaults builds 4 steps x 3 layers with steps 1-3 carrying 2268/1736/1774 cells, 126-246 mm extrusion per step layer, and a rendered height map that shows the photo; the file was never the problem. The remaining field explanation: a watcher process that silently survived the update restart (update.sh's pkill/systemctl can fail without sudo or on root zombies). Now: every generated file's first line carries `; R2-GEN core R2-2026-10-22.125 (R166)`, the watcher publishes `postproc_build` to save_variables (LCD-visible) and screams at boot when the macros on disk are newer than itself, and update.sh PROVES the restart (every running r2_postproc process must have started after the update unpacked + the build stamp must match this release once klippy is back). | **.123 (R163)** | **MCU shutdown root cause fixed: the LED animation flooded the serial bus.** Second 'Timer too close' ten minutes into the .121 litho test print - with RAM fine (~100MB) and a SPARSE file, so neither moves nor memory were the trigger. The klippy.log told the real story: the shutdown send-queue dump held 100 messages, 75 of them neopixel_send/neopixel_update; 28503 'Neopixel update did not succeed' (failed readbacks, resent!) in one day; the animated r2_printing gradient streamed LED frames at 24 fps through the whole print while the motion buffer held only ~1.2s - a 0.2-0.9s GC pause on the Pi Zero 2 W then pushed a move past its deadline. Fix: the printing state is a STATIC blue now (klipper led.py only transmits CHANGED colors - static = zero LED traffic while printing), every other effect dropped 24 -> 12 fps (half the load, half the readback retries, visually identical). LED reference in MANUAL/PDF updated with the reasoning. |
| **.122 (R162)** | **Lithophane FLAT finally prints PHOTOS + host-overload hardening.** (1) **FIELD FIX (the picture never printed):** the step regions of a FLAT lithophane hatch from the first to the last cell CENTER - single-row blocks (nearly ALL blocks of a photo, every image row differs) collapsed to a ZERO-height rect: no fill, the file was base plate + frame + faint diagonal lines (.110 'just a flat plate' and .121 'lithophane still does not work' were THIS bug, partially misdiagnosed as shallow defaults). Multi-row blocks lost their outer half-rows too (52% fill). The hatch rects now use the FULL cell extent - measured on the user's Bun2.jpg: step layers 63.5mm filament before -> 168mm (step 1) / 108mm (step 2), 100% of the level cell mass, correct terracing. The color picture generator carried the SAME bug (fill + ironing, 4 sites) - fixed together. New audit guard: a PHOTO-like synthetic image (organic single-row bands) must reach 70-135% fill coverage per step + terrace, plus a colorpic band-fill check - the old sketch-based tests could never see it. (2) **FIELD FIX 'File fix: blocked - a print is running' while standing in Pre-Print:** the one-shot busy check killed the travel fix 3s after the macro had already released the start dialog (CANCEL_PRINT_BASE + mode=idle) - on the memory-pressured Pi the moonraker status lagged. Now the R2 mode is the authority (mode idle = no print) and a contradicting print_stats is re-checked for up to 10s before the fix gives up. (3) **Host-overload chain closed (the .121 'Timer too close' MCU shutdown during a print with a background litho build):** a busy-probe TIMEOUT read as 'not busy' - the litho built DURING the print; the probe retries once now. And generator builds wait below 60MB RAM free like they wait for the printer ('[queue] X waits: only NMB RAM free'), because PIL + gcode rewrite on a Pi Zero 2 W starves klippy. Battery: render 1694/1694 (+3 R162 guards), audit 82/0 (+5 photo-fill/terracing/colorpic checks), validator clean, journeys 1856/0, stress 46639/0, megatest 157/0, goldens 23/23, r143 68/0, systemtest 221/0, dialects 13/13, setup-sim 10/10, spell 0. |
| **.121 (R161)** | **Layer-2 takeover made reliable (field report: bed/hotend/fan did not switch from layer 2).** Root causes: (1) the layer-2 boundary rewrote temps ONLY when a user override was armed - files without their own M104/M140 (generated files, range excerpts) kept the layer-1 values for the whole print; (2) AS SLICED ignored your armed L2 override completely; (3) AS SLICED even forced the material preset over the file's OWN M104/M140 from layer 2 (not 1:1 at all); (4) M109/M190 carried the same force-material rule. New unified semantics: **layer 2 = your armed override > the file's value > the material preset (while L1 rules are ON), in BOTH start modes**, for marked files (LAYER_MARK) and unmarked ones (Z tracker) alike; interceptors consistent - AS SLICED shapes layer 1 with the material L1 rule and runs layer 2 truly 1:1 except your armed override (single "M104: 215C -> YOUR L2 override -> 240C (AS SLICED, layer 2)" log), AS LEGACY rewrites a layer-2 command only when your override is armed; every heater write idempotent (only when the target moves > 0.5 C - no redundant writes, no double logs); the held layer-1 fan returns at the boundary (M106 S<file value>) and the material fan scale re-applies. Manual ch. 8 rewritten to describe exactly this ladder. Battery: render 1691/1691 (+15 R161 matrix), validator clean, journeys 1856/0, stress 46639/0, megatest 157/0, goldens 23/23, audit 77/0 + r143 68/0, systemtest 221/0, dialects 13/13, setup-sim 10/10, spell 0. |
| **.120 (R160)** | **Display-native names, double-press generations, real values.** (1) **File names without the 00_ prefix**: generator outputs are `Vnn_Name.gcode` - the version LEADS, three more characters for the name on the 15-character display, one number per output folder, never reused; all three legacy formats (00_Name_Vnn, 00_Vnn_Name, Vnn_Name) keep counting in the same sequence, so nothing reuses a number after the update. (2) **Every Create row confirms by DOUBLE PRESS**: first press arms ("Again = start" + soft click, 10s window), the second press starts the build with a short start chirp (r2_gen); a waiting start dialog cancels itself QUIETLY (nothing prints unasked, no sad tone over the chirp). Console/Mainsail stay single-shot - a typed command IS the confirmation. (3) **File info / Fixes split**: Pre-Print > File info is pure information + 16 feature ON/OFF rows (`OW [on] 60mm/s SAPF` - one glance shows state AND values, markers `>`/`*` say what a press does); everything that REWRITES the file moved to Pre-Print > Fixes (retract verdict + fix, RetFix ON/OFF, Vol->mm, Segment fix, flow cap, travel speed/accel/fix - the .118 duplicates gone). (4) **Real values instead of ?**: generated files carry measured FS_/FA_ per feature + SLICER=R2_GEN + RETRACT_STATUS=fw in their _FILE_INFO - the Spd/Acc/activator rows read exactly those; temps fall back to the material preset (generated files heat via PRINT_START), PA to the printer global, the Ret row shows the firmware G10/G11 values. (5) **Preview card resets temps per selection** - the previous file's numbers never linger (R74 rule, temps edition). (6) The file census walks the generator SUBFOLDERS (relative names, gen flag knows Vnn_ + legacy). (7) **FIELD FIX texture/plot sources from subfolders**: the watcher stripped the folder from the selected source - a texture on any file from Texture/, Labels/ (the TX output auto-selects itself!) died with one error beep and no file; sources keep their relative path now. (8) **FIELD FIX wedged states**: a start that died mid-flow (e.g. a homing error) left mode='starting' FOREVER - every generator silently requeued, the LEDs kept blinking, _UI_SOUND stayed mute; the _STATE_WATCHER heals stuck modes (3 min after the heaters settle / 20 min hard limit), PRINT_END does all cleanup BEFORE its presentation move and only moves with homed axes, and _UI_SOUND gates on the REAL print state. (9) **Per-job sounds**: a queued job chirps (r2_gen) when it actually starts building, reports WHY it waits after 90 s, and a crash beeps r2_error - range and texture builds now run in the job queue too (they froze the poller). (10) **No-overwrite naming**: if a planned name exists at write time, the version gets a letter tail (V05 taken -> V05b - user rule), the scan counts letters so the sequence moves on; range names carry 10 source characters (was 3), the source's own version prefix stripped from the tag (V05_Vase_klein -> V06_Vaseklein_L7-355, not 00V). Multi-plate outputs (frame A/B, kit A/B/C) carry the plate letter DIRECTLY behind the version - V01_A_Frame_..., V02_B_Frame_... - so the role survives the 15-character display cut (the old tail suffix was invisible next to its sibling). (11) **BOOT FIX (first .120 zip halted at boot)**: [delayed_gcode] takes only gcode/initial_duration - the new _GEN_ARM_CLEAR section carried a description: line; removed, and the config validator now rejects ANY invalid section option statically (boot guard test in the render suite). Tokens parse in ANY order now; your vase gets 209,257/209,257 scattered vertices, Z and token order untouched. Verified against your upload: TX now differs from the source on every layer. (3) **The 'two wrong features' in Pre-Print**: the range's _FILE_INFO said TYPES=INFILL-SOLID,BRIM (raft+brim only) - a spiral states its ;TYPE: once, ABOVE the excerpt window, so the scan missed WALL-OUTER. (9) **Generator speed image**: every print generator runs 60mm/s + 1500 accel, travels 180mm/s + 8000 accel - with an MCU-aware rate cap (micro segments slow down exactly as far as the 400-moves/s serial budget needs; long brim/wall moves get the full speed). | **.114 (R153)** | **Geometry tagging perfected: the vase rule, the brim rule, and the answer to 'does fuzzy skin work now?'.** The R111 geometry classifier (loops->walls by nesting, parallel lines->infill, layer 1->bottom, last->top - it already tagged UNTAGGED files with derived APPLY_TYPE lines at confidence 0.7) learns the field rules: (1) **Vase rule** - a run of extruding moves that carry a slowly rising Z is a spiral; after the bottom ONE feature remains and that is OUTER WALL, always (no more fragment 'layers' from the 0.05mm Z micro-steps). (2) **Brim rule** - a layer-1 wall loop with NO successor on layer 2 is a BRIM (walls continue upward, brim does not); the real outer wall, nested inside the brim ring, is promoted back to WALL-OUTER. (3) **Bug fix**: derived APPLY_TYPE lines were keyed by the RAW line index while the writer counts filtered lines - they landed next to the wrong lines or nowhere. End-to-end proof with an untagged vase file: brim ring -> BRIM, real wall -> WALL-OUTER, 12 bottom lines -> SURFACE-BOTTOM, all 1428 spiral moves -> WALL-OUTER, and the fuzzy texture on that processed file shifts exactly 1432 vertices (the whole spiral + the outer wall, brim untouched) - fuzzy skin now works for tagged AND untagged vase prints. This is the foundation the user sketched: geometry understanding for perfect tagging (also the basis for object localization / plotting onto printed objects in Z - next steps). Battery: coverage 126/0 (+3), render 1632/1632, goldens 23/23, systemtest/megatest/stress/journeys/dialects/r91/spell/audit/setup-sim re-run. |

Now: independent guards, a cache only counts when moonraker says 200, failed pushes RETRY every cycle ('[sdfiles] push failed - retrying next cycle' in the log), and while the list is empty the menu shows a visible 'List empty - info' fallback instead of a dead menu. (2) **Fuzzy skin / bumps in vase mode**: Orca spells the spiral wall ';TYPE:Wall outer' while normal walls say 'Outer wall' - the texture only matched the latter, so a vase carried the effect on its first (and last) normal layer and NONE along the spiral. The texture now understands the full alias set (Outer wall / Wall outer / WALL-OUTER / External perimeter) AND reads the canonical APPLY_TYPE markers the post-processor injects - verified: 327/327 spiral moves textured (fuzzy AND bumps). (3) **Litho FLAT deeper by default**: 3 layers per step instead of 2 (2.4mm total relief at the defaults) - the '.110 just a flat plate' impression was (also) the shallow 1.6mm default; the header now states 'Xmm total relief, Ymm per step (more = deeper)' and the StepL menu entry shows mm. | **.108 (R147)** | **HOTFIX (.107-era field log): ghost fix requests re-fired on every watcher restart + duplicate-watcher survival made loud.** Root cause of the boot-time 'File fix: AxtBunny.png.gcode not found' beep loop and the 'Travel accel DONE' burst right after an update: the three newer fix channels (flowcap / travelacc / segments) were READ in the watch loop but never added to the NONCE_KEYS priming set - every watcher restart (update, reboot) saw their stored nonces as 'new' and re-fired the OLD request; a request for a long-deleted file beeped 'not found' on every restart, forever (the not-found branch never cleared the channel variable). Same bug class as .99/.133c, third and hopefully last occurrence - now a hard r143 audit rule: every nonce channel the watch loop reads MUST be in NONCE_KEYS. Fixed: all three channels primed (a restart adopts them as seen - nothing re-fires), and a ghost request now DIES at its first 'not found' (the channel's fix_file variable is cleared + honest 'request dropped' message). Plus: a duplicate watcher that SURVIVES the stop attempt (root cron / rc.local relic - it doubles every push and can drown the Pi Zero host into 'Timer too close' shutdowns, as in this field log) now gets a LOUD console RESPOND with the exact stop command, rate-limited to once per hour. Battery: r143 65/0/0 (+4 R147), validator clean, systemtest 221/0, megatest 157/163/0, render 1632/1632, goldens 23/23, dialects 13/13, stress/journeys/coverage/spell/audit re-run. |

| **.106 (R145)** | **HOTFIX (.105 field report: PRINTER HALTED AT BOOT)** - `Option 'type' in section 'menu __main __octoprint' must be specified`. The .104 menu restructure hid the stock Klipper menus (OctoPrint / Temperature / Setup) with `enable: {False}`-only override sections, assuming Klipper merges options into its built-in menu.cfg definitions. It does NOT: menu.py loads its own menu.cfg as a SEPARATE config and reads every `[menu]` section of the main config standalone (`cfg.get('type')` has no default; a same-name section REPLACES the whole stock item - 'hides previous menuitem declaration'). The enable-only sections therefore had no type = boot halt on the first real printer (.104/.105 had never booted outside the sim). Fixed: all three overrides now carry FULL definitions (type: list + name + enable {False}) - valid standalone AND a clean replacement of the stock entries; SD Card / Control / Tune untouched as before. The config validator's R135 stock-menu whitelist (which exempted exactly these sections from the type check - the gap that let this ship) is REPLACED by a hard rule: any enable-only [menu] section fails, and the render suite scans every menu section in lcd_menues.cfg for a missing type. Battery: render 1622/1622 (R135 stock-hide test reworked into a hard R145 regression), validator clean (new rule), systemtest 221/0, megatest 157/163/0, audit 77/0, r143 61/0/0, coverage 104/0, spell 0, setup-sim 10/10. |
| **.105 (R144)** | **The audit round - R143's user-perspective audit found 7 findings, all fixed.** **Tagging canonized (F1-F3)**: every slicer's `;TYPE:` dialect now flows through one `_canon_type()` mapper - `Wall Outer`/`;TYPE :` (space before colon)/`Support base`/`tree support`/mixed case all land on the canonical names, unknown types become CUSTOM instead of silently masquerading, and our own restored markers (INFILL-SPARSE) pass through verbatim (the 2nd restore used to log a wrong WALL-OUTER) - wired at all 6 call sites: main pass, travel-acc pass, range excerpts, v1 upgrade counter. **LAYER_MARK anchors right (F4)**: the mark now sits on the layer's FIRST EXTRUDING line (source-indexed), not the Z-rise - restoration drift is constant and pinned by test; golden refs re-froze (only marker positions moved, proven by diff). **Freeze channels complete (F5/F6)**: `plot_cal_nonce` + `range_nonce` were missing from the restart freeze - a watcher restart could re-fire old plot/range jobs; both frozen now. **SET_LAYER_RANGE busy-guard (F6b)**: refuses while printing/paused with a warn notice (only when idle). **_UI_SOUND (F7)**: the 20 menu confirmation beeps (labels/bricks/colorpic/figures/range) route through a new macro that stays silent while printing, paused or changing filament. **r143 audit bench hardened** into 61 hard regression checks (0 findings left). Battery: r143 61/0/0, dialects 13/13, goldens 23/23, megatest 157/163/0, render 1622/1622, systemtest 221/0, stress 46599, journeys 1816, coverage 104, spell 0, audit 77/0, validator clean, setup-sim 10/10. |
| **.104 (R135)** | **The menu restructure - one screen, one job.** Nothing was lost - an inventory test (tools/r135_menu_golden.txt, 1657 pre-restructure paths) proves every vanished path is a documented merge/rename. Pre-Print: **Offset X/Y/Z** at the very top (X/Y/Z plus fine rows with HALVED 0.05 mm steps, realtime MOVE=1), File info directly below - the fixes (RetFix ON/file, travel accel, flow cap + values) now a submenu of File - Profiles next. **Velocity** (ex-Motion): retraction + accel/vel/MCR/corner + Z-Hop (the duplicated top-level Z-Hop entries are gone). The four quick lists **Spd/Acc/PA/Flw per feat** absorb the old top-level Speeds/Accels/Flow menus and now lead with their GLOBALS (1st-layer speed, global accel + ON, global PA + smooth + File PA, global flow + ON) plus a Now row naming the active feature - identical in Live Tuning and Pre-Print. The Plotter sections (PEN FILE, pen speed, Pen Z, shifts) moved to the END of both panels and appear ONLY for plot files (_PREVIEW.plot or file_type plot). Klipper stock menus **OctoPrint / Temperature / Setup hidden** (enable {False} overrides - the documented menu.cfg merge; everything exists in the R2 menus), SD Card browser / Control / Tune stay. Bonus fixes: the dead duplicate temp-tower entry ([menu __maint __tt __go] twice) - "Start tower" is now __gen and reachable; a latent format bug in the new offset rows was caught by the render suite ('%.2f' not '.2f'). Docs: MANUAL ch. 3.4 + 14 menu tables rewritten, INSTALL updated. Battery: megatest 157/163/0, render 1621 (+10 R135), systemtest 221/0, stress/journeys/coverage/spell/audit re-run after the version bump, validator clean (stock-override whitelist). |
| **.103 (R134)** | **Plot-on-selection - the plotter becomes a choice, not a side effect**: uploading an image/text/PDF never converts anything anymore. The old flow auto-converted EVERY matching upload (`*.png`, `*.jpg.gcode`, `*.txt` ...) in the background - including every plain image uploaded as a litho/colorpic source (field log .101: `AxtBunny.png` grew an unrequested `AxtBunny.png.gcode` twin) - unpredictable background work on the Zero 2 W plus clutter in the file list. NEW flow: upload the PLAIN file (`photo.png`, `notes.txt`, `plan.pdf`, `drawing.svg` - the `.gcode` tail is dead), SELECT it in the file list (LCD or Mainsail) - `SDCARD_PRINT_FILE` detects the source extension, switches to plot mode and bumps `plot_nonce`; the watcher generates `00_Plot_<name>_V01.gcode` on demand like every other generator (one job at a time, never while printing, Maintenance > gen-out decides selected-for-start vs preview only for the 3D view). Pen mode stays unheated (no bed, no hotend, no purge), FILAMENT mode heats to the material preset - unchanged `_PLOT_START` semantics. A busy printer refuses the selection with a clear message instead of ever BASE-starting an image as g-code; an already-converted plot g-code selected again is re-selected, never regenerated; a raw `.gcode`-tailed upload (old habit) is left byte-identical with a rename hint. Litho/ColorPic outputs never collided (`00_Litho_*`/`00_ColorPic_*`) - but their SOURCE images stopped spawning plot twins. Docs (MANUAL ch. 5.3, INSTALL) updated; behavioral tests pin: no twin on any scan pass, hint not a rewrite, selection flow, busy refuse. Battery: megatest 157/163/0, render 1611, systemtest 221, stress 46596, journeys 1807, coverage 99, spell 1506/0, audit 0, validator clean. |
| **.102 (R133i)** | **Field log .101: the takeover guard itself was the bug - a one-shot must never kill**: the .101 takeover hook ran for EVERY invocation, so an SSH `--file-check` diagnostic killed the LIVE systemd watcher (PID 574, reported as 'stale duplicate' - which it was NOT) and every generator fell silent until the next boot: litho/ColorPic presses echoed their settings but nothing was ever built. The takeover now runs in `--watch` daemon mode ONLY; a manual `--watch` never kills the SUPERVISED systemd instance (cgroup check - the manual start exits politely, the supervised one wins); `--file-check` now reports the watcher state and warns LOUDLY when no watcher is running. Plus the runtime hole the same log exposed: pre-.98 zombies never write the lock, so a zombie starting AFTER the good watcher stayed invisible forever - NEW `_watch_duplicate_sweep()` scans /proc once a minute (no subprocess, cheap on the Zero 2 W) and stops ANY other r2_postproc process, any version, lock or not; a ROOT duplicate (root cron / rc.local relic) gets a loud how-to-stop respond (user pi cannot signal root). update.sh tries `sudo -n pkill` first. Fixed the display float artifact `3 x 0.6 = 1.7999999999999998mm` (in Jinja a trailing `\|round` binds to the right operand only - the whole product is now parenthesized and renders `1.8mm`). Behavioral tests pin both: a fake watcher + lock survives an ordinary `--file-check` untouched, and the thickness echo rounds. Battery: megatest 157/163/0, render 1605, systemtest 221, stress 46596, journeys 1807, coverage 93, spell 1502/0, audit 0, validator clean. |
| **.101 (R133h)** | **THE SHUTDOWN killer is dead - one watcher, ever (field log .100)**: a zombie pre-.98 watcher (started manually once, outside systemd) survived every update, ran parallel to the systemd watcher (doubling every console message) and pushed its stale kit-source list as a BARE unquoted value - `ast.literal_eval` SyntaxError = `Internal error on command:SAVE_VARIABLE` = printer shutdown (struck in idle; the same push mid-print would have killed the print). NOT a concurrency/rate problem: Klipper serializes all gcode in one mutex, a single malformed push suffices - micro-pauses would have changed nothing. Fixes: `_watch_lock_takeover()` (lock + loud takeover: RESPOND, SIGTERM, 5s, SIGKILL, atexit cleanup), update.sh pkills stray watchers before the systemd restart, a SAVE_VARIABLE pipeline lint (every rendered macro save, every menu save and every watcher push format goes through Klipper's EXACT shlex + literal_eval pipeline - a bare value can never ship again), and the gen_files push is now a real list (was a string; the LCD would have shown single-character slots). Verify after updating: update.sh ends with the watcher census ('exactly 1 r2-postproc watcher (PID ...)'); by hand use the self-match-proof `pgrep -af '[r]2_postproc.py'` (exactly ONE process - under sudo a bare pattern lists the sudo+pgrep pair itself, .136d), then FIRMWARE_RESTART. Battery: megatest 157/163/0, render 1603, systemtest 221, stress 46596, journeys 1807, coverage 84, spell 1502/0, audit 0, validator clean. |

FLAT + ColorPic no longer mirror the image (the mirror is the STANDING backlit recipe) and print with the image top at the BACK - the picture reads correct from the printer front. Quantization now snaps to the NEAREST level (a clean 3-tone image keeps its gray even with the contrast boost on). Semantic coverage checks with a marker image pin polarity, orientation and the layer math. Battery: megatest 157/163/0, render 1601, systemtest 221, stress 46596, journeys 1807, coverage 84, spell 1502/0, audit 0, validator clean. |

Every nonce channel is now primed AND a coverage check forbids ever adding an unprimed one. **Naming overhaul**: the Vers setting is retired - versions bump themselves (V01, V02, ...; regenerating NEVER overwrites, a deleted highest number is reused), file names use the FULL source name (no [:8] cut) plus generator info (Figure names carry SINGLE/PARTS). **Tools > Gen files**: every 00_ output on the display - press to select (start dialog catches it), Delete file = 2-press; only 00_ outputs are ever deletable. Fixed: the litho Delete-image slots were wired to LITHO_CREATE (creating instead of deleting!). Journeys simulate the selection via the card. |

NEW GEN_QUEUE_CANCEL (LCD Tools > Cancel job queue) drops pending jobs - the .95/.96 chaos had queued a pile that kept writing files after every printer restart (FIRMWARE_RESTART does not restart the watcher). |

Generator jobs now run through a single deduplicated queue (a duplicate press answers 'already running'), the worker requeues while a print runs (print always first), the scan pauses while a job works, micro-pauses yield the CPU in the litho/ColorPic hot loops, and the R75 niceness stays (klipper always wins). Also fixed: the kit success message crashed on a list (%d) since R125 - every finished kit LOOKED like an error; kit source picking plot files; stale kit AUTO menu texts; MOVERATE stamping moved from file selection (full-file scan froze the LCD) into the job worker. |

(2) AS LEGACY takeover could cold-extrude ("Extrude below minimum temp" — the file's own prime lines run before the pause engages); _LEGACY_PREP now waits for the hotend target BEFORE pausing and the M109 preheat cap floors at min_extrude_temp+5. (3) Layer-range excerpts keep the source's EXCLUDE_OBJECT_DEFINE blocks, so KAMP meshes adaptively instead of falling back to a full-bed mesh. |
| **.53 (R69)** | **HOTFIX**: `SAVE_VARIABLE` could SHUT DOWN the printer — Klipper strips the quotes (POSIX shlex) and only catches ValueError, so any string with a space, an apostrophe, an empty value or the label queue's pipes raised SyntaxError = "Internal error on command" = shutdown. All R63/R64 string saves (label text/queue, plot-name editor, reso/shaper/plot-file stores, 4 watcher pushes) moved to the escaped quote form + quote/backslash validation + an `_sv_line` builder in the watcher. New test gate replays Klipper's exact parser over every cfg line and sweeps the whole charset through the label editor. Also removed `;` from the charset (Klipper cuts every command line at the first `;` — it could never be saved). |

Fixed: comments stripped before parsing, regex requires a digit, per-token guard; phantom params from comment text (`; Ender-3` → `R=-3`) gone too. Affected files are picked up automatically on the next service start. |

Fixed with the proven `.split()` pattern; new test gate compiles ALL ~1565 boot-compiled templates (macro gcode + menu name/enable/gcode) and renders every menu template — this error class can never ship again. |

## License

GPL-3.0-or-later — see [LICENSE](LICENSE). Built field-log driven and test
gated: [DEVELOPMENT.md](DEVELOPMENT.md) tells the story,
[COMPATIBILITY.md](COMPATIBILITY.md) what it takes to run on your printer.

[![tests](https://github.com/Raz-666/klipper-e3-r2d2/actions/workflows/tests.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/Raz-666/klipper-e3-r2d2/actions/workflows/tests.yml)

| **.136aa (R229)** | **The vocabulary REST LIST - the last open items of the slicer-family compilation (user question from the .136v audit round).** (1) *Non-extrusion annotations are inert*: KISSlicer marks retract/travel moves as `; feature Destring` and the seam point as `; feature Seam`; G-code viewers show travel/retract/tool-change/color-change/pause events as pseudo types. A seam sits INSIDE a wall loop - as a feature block it would type the following WALL extrusions TRAVEL/CUSTOM and the wall overrides would silently stop applying there. The TYPE regex now rejects these words (whole-line anchored, near-misses like 'wipe wall' unaffected): the feature context persists through them. Direct `_canon_type` callers get a map safety net (all -> TRAVEL, the established no-extrusion canon). (2) *Purge/prime structures are one class*: prime wall, wipe wall (CORRECTION: skirt since R225 - a wipe wall IS a purge structure), prime pillar, wiping tower, purge block, purge line, prime blob -> PRIME-TOWER. (3) *Rest assignments*: belt lead -> skirt (belt-printer adhesion strip), brim ears -> brim, painted support -> support, the raft stack -> bottom surface (the existing 'raft' approximation). (4) *Infill pattern names* (tpms/tpms-d/tpms-fk, octet, tetrahedral, stars, lateral honeycomb, lattice, spiral inset, tri-hexagon) are patterns, not features - all reduce to sparse infill (R216 stage-7 rule). Field context: .136z confirmed working in the field (clean boot log, no spam/storm/banner - the export shows a boring healthy boot). CI: r229_vocab_rest 46/0 (inertness table + near-misses + end-to-end: a wall->seam->destring->wall file keeps ONE wall feature), r225 re-pinned (wipe wall correction), tier 27 tools. |
| **.136z (R228)** | **The .136y field log: the Orca vase printed (R226 works!), but layer 1 became a pause storm that ended in an MCU shutdown.** Field evidence: full-bed mesh with the CENTER-only hint, probe-safe prime line, 20x20mm park box - all R226 degradations visible and working; then quick-pause/resume churn on layer 1, "State re-synced: mode was stuck in 'paused'" after EVERY resume, third resume -> MCU 'Timer too close' shutdown, and every subsequent reconcile push re-printed the whole shutdown banner (2x per 6 s). The user asked whether the motion sensor's start chirps ('nothing conveyed') armed the pauses - in THIS log they did not (the pause reason was 'quick pause' = the LCD item), but the chirps are real and now harmless. (1) *Watcher resume race*: after our RESUME the mode flips to 'printing' a moment before print_stats follows (M24 updates it only as the file read resumes - seconds on a slow first-layer file); the watcher's bypassed-pause branch sampled that gap and knocked the mode back to 'paused' (the print kept running, the desync heal needed ~10 s - "stuck in paused" after every resume). The branch now needs THREE consecutive ticks: a pause that bypassed us (Mainsail button) persists, the resume gap never does. (2) *Filament sensor grace windows* (the user's question): _PRIME_LINE arms 30 s at print start, _RESUME_COMMON arms 20 s after the refill moves - _FILAMENT_RUNOUT reports chatter instead of arming a pause while the motion wheel lags behind the building pressure; a real runout re-fires after the window. (3) *Pause NOW arm expiry*: the two-press guard had NO timeout - one armed click stayed armed forever and any later single click (or an EMI ghost enter on the 12864 encoder) paused the print; the arm expires after 5 s. (4) *Banner storm + burst*: the daemon reconcile is rate-limited to one attempt per 10 s (the print-start backlog scan amplified 8 pushes in 8 s) and gated on klippy_state=='ready' - no push into a shutdown klippy, where each one re-printed the full 'Timer too close' banner to every client. Also flagged (user side, not in-repo): `Unknown command:"_M204"` twice at the prime line - no sender exists in this repo, an old private macro on the Pi references it. CI: r228_pause_storm 13/0, macro_render +7 R228 renders (watcher tick 1/3, runout grace on/off, grace arming in prime line + resume), r81 re-frozen (stamp class), tier 26 tools. |
| **.136y (R227)** | **The .136x field log, part 2: the reconciliation sprayed, the day export ran old code, the build stamp lied.** (1) *The reconciliation spew*: the R226 gen_busy reconciliation fired EVERY watcher pass (~6 s - 'stale gen_busy cleared' 8x in 42 s, no VALUE=1 set in between, console spray without end). Root cause: `_read_vars()` returns STRINGS (its R150 doc says so - every other caller converts), and the R226 test `if rv.get('gen_busy')` was STRING truthiness: `'0'` (from `variable_gen_busy: False`) is a non-empty string = TRUE in Python, so the test could never see the healthy flag. Fix: the value is PARSED for real ('1'/'true'/'on'/'yes'), and the notice fires ONCE per stale episode (latch in _JOB_STATE; a refusing klippy retries SILENTLY - a down klippy must not spray the console; the latch rearms when a pass sees the flag healthy or a real job owns it). Print starts were never blocked by the spam - the macros read the live klipper variable. (2) *The day export that ran old code*: the field's 78-byte klippy-day.txt was byte-identical to the .136w bug - because web/r2_menu.py is loaded at MOONRAKER startup and update.sh restarted klipper + watcher but not moonraker; the export ran the old filter until the PI was rebooted by hand (the 3h export after the reboot came out perfect: 1871 lines, dumps skipped). update.sh now restarts moonraker itself (after the census - that talks TO moonraker). (3) *The stale build stamp*: the boot said 'R2-2026-11-08.136x (R223)' - POSTPROC_BUILD had not been bumped since R223. Now names its round. CI: r227_reconcile_fix 17/0 (parse table over every representation Klipper writes + latch + update.sh), r226 re-pinned (7 releases), macro_render re-pinned, tier 25 tools. |
| **.136x (R226)** | **The .136w field log: three bugs from the printer, all fixed at the root.** (1) *The Orca CENTER-only crash* (`V03_TL_new_S75-75_TX.gcode`, 405 layers, single-wall vase - the print start ABORTED): OrcaSlicer/Bambu write `EXCLUDE_OBJECT_DEFINE NAME=… CENTER=x,y` with NO polygon; KAMP's SMART_PARK runs `objects|map(attribute='polygon')` -> jinja UndefinedError -> printer in ERROR, three SMART_PARK errors in the log, no print possible. Three-layer fix: the watcher MEASURES the object outline from its moves (`_upgrade_center_excludes` - per-object bbox between EXCLUDE_OBJECT_START/END, single unmeasured object falls back to the global move bbox, unmeasurable stays untouched, `POLYGON=[[…]]` appended atomically + idempotently, 64KB head early-exit, seen-key cache); our own placement macros (_PRIME_LINE purge box, _PARK_HEAD, _PURGE_STRIP) read `o.polygon is defined` with a 20×20mm center-box fallback; the KAMP call sites (SMART_PARK, adaptive mesh) are guarded - any polygonless object -> standard park + full-bed mesh via the renamed native `_BED_MESH_CALIBRATE` with a RESPOND hint instead of a crash. (2) *The gen_busy zombie*: the flag lives in variables.cfg and SURVIVES klippy restarts; the R223 boot-drop only ran when the WATCHER restarted, and a release could fail while klippy was down - the persisted 1 blocked every print start (user had to clear it by hand). Every scan pass now RECONCILES flag vs reality (no thread alive, no queue busy -> clear loudly: RESPOND + M117 "Queue free"). | **.136n (R216)** | **The .136m field feedback round - the web page becomes a machine panel, and the feature recognition learns the last two lessons.** (1) *WebUI like Mainsail*: file renames, paging and pickers in the browser, brick scaling, and a **Machine panel** - every system button (firmware/host restart, shutdown, save config, emergency stop) in ONE dedicated section with the same 2-press guards as the LCD; **log emptying is a checkbox selection menu** (nothing wipes just because the page was open); the live poll consolidated to one 3 s cycle with a command-index guard (no double commands after a lag spike). (2) *The travel golden rule*: no travel may ever CROSS freshly printed plastic - a spatial hash of the current layer's ink answers per travel (endpoint touches and parallel neighbor lines never count), retract+Z-hop only where it is really needed, no matter how short the jump. (3) *Bed screws, Mainsail's flow on the LCD* (Maintenance > Bed Screws): Start homes and moves to screw 1, Next walks screw by screw with clock notation (01:20 = 1 turn + 20 minutes, CW/CCW), the display shows the measured deviation of the current position, :ok when a screw sits, Round starts a fresh pass, Done reports the max deviation. (4) *PA pattern*: LINES or the 5-stroke ANGLE sawtooth, optional 0-10 layer solid BASE, every pattern inside a 2 mm frame (also on the base) - the enclosure marks the test area and makes corners readable. (5) *Geometry generator* (Generator > Geometry): TRIANGLE/SQUARE/POLY 3-12/CYLINDER, walls 1-8 or wall thickness in mm, infill 0-100 %, WALLS=1 = vase mode (spiral, no top); output V01_Shape_... in Shapes/. (6) *Taper + Twist*: taper is an OPTION of Scale X/Y (Top X/Y 25-300 %, factor linear in Z over the extruding span, E per move with the local factor, name_S100-100T60-60.gcode); Twist is its own tool (-720..720 deg around the object center, E verbatim, bed guard on the circumscribed circle, name_TWR90.gcode). (7) *STL export field-proven*: watertight mesh, opens in Blender/PrusaSlicer/Cura/Fusion (OBJ as an alternative), start sound + LCD notify when done. (8) *Feature recognition stage 7 - every infill variant + the layers check each other*: the 3D families (Gyroid, 3D-Lattice, Lightning, Cross-Cubic, Honeycomb) and the classic patterns (Tri-Hexagon, Zig-Zag, Rectilinear, Concentric, Octagram, Hilbert, Wiggle) all map to INFILL-SPARSE (solid/top/bottom tokens still win, support grids stay SUPPORT); an open CURVED path bathed in material on both sides is 3D infill, not a wall - the 8 mm far probe sees the 2-6 mm line spacing, a monotone-turning arc stays a wall (R181 pinned); solid hatch now needs the neighbors' vote in MULTIPLE PASSES (elimination principle): the next layer's picture flips a candidate to SURFACE-TOP when nothing - not even a wall ring - prints above its region (a thin ring above = the model goes on = stays solid, the R214 field rule; only the uppermost of a stacked top shell becomes the top surface; the final pass at end of file flips the rest). (9) *Two new file fixes* (Pre-Print > Fixes): **Quality** applies the travel golden rule to a sliced file - retract/hop blocks around travels that cross NO ink drop (the feature stage contributes ONLY the travel detection, no feature is re-tagged), crossings keep their armor, naked crossings are named; **Speed** merges consecutive travels into direct ways (position-true) and skips the ironing pass (G92 stitch keeps absolute-E files exact). (c) *String art, seven field findings*: E is filament mm again (the old values were volumetric scale - the print came out ~2.4x over-extruded); the file carries its temps (preheat fell back to filament-swap level); each strand runs as a 2 mm BOW in Z and the strand travel hops over the bows; pull direction alternates; consecutive starts keep >= 15 deg apart; SHAPE=RECT with the photo-size presets (9x13..18x24, the Frames sizes) + custom W/H; the ring is 2 walls + 3 solid bottom + 3 solid top + 5% sparse between (RingH 1.2-6 mm adjustable). FOUR loads piled onto the Pi Zero 2 W while klippy needed its reactor for just-in-time move scheduling: (1) the watcher scan re-analyzed + REWROTE every generated file on EVERY pass (the R212 meta hook had no change guard - mtimes churned, the SD card was written endlessly); gcode_meta.finalize now carries an R2-META body signature (md5 over the file body): unchanged files return 'same' after one read - zero analyze, zero thumbnail render, zero write (r81 re-frozen with proof: all 13 mr fixtures = old reference + exactly the one sig line, pr untouched). (2) gen_files keeps its pushed ORDER while the SET is unchanged (the equal-mtime rotation behind the flip-flop). (3) the push pass re-probes _printer_busy right BEFORE pushing - a pass that started before the print click no longer fires SAVE_VARIABLEs during homing (the log had pushes at :12/:20 while homing ran since :08; shutdown at :24). (4) the web page's live poll queried configfile:None - klippy serialized the ENTIRE config text every 3 s while the page was open (the 0.197 s stall that killed the 14:18 homing was exactly this query); now 'settings' only (section names are all the menus test) plus a 25 s start quiet window on the page after start-type commands (live poll pauses, console keeps flowing). HOST_CHECK now also counts klippy's OWN symptoms from klippy.log ('Reactor: 134 stalls up to 0.262 s + 50 failed LED updates - klippy was BLOCKED repeatedly; the next homing can hit Timer too close') so 'load 1.5, Throttle NONE' stops looking healthy. Without live data: raw jinja labels (the save_variables text), ZERO conditional hiding (OctoPrint/Temperature/Live-Tuning/Start? all visible - LCD parity was paper), the menu order looked wrong because hidden menus were not hidden. (2) Even WITH live data nothing would have been hidden: enable strings ship WITH their braces and the evaluator parsed that as a dict literal -> exception -> null -> never hidden - found by making the enable-parity test strict. (3) The sender would have POSTed raw templates - 968 of 1964 commands carry jinja; klipper menu EVALUATES item gcode before running it, so the page must too. (4) JS/Python string semantics differ: String([]) is empty in JS but str([]) is [] in Python - replace/upper/join on list values silently rendered wrong. Back navigation: the up button + clickable breadcrumb. | **.136h (R209)** | **THE UR-BUG, found at last: the web-LCD page's JavaScript had NEVER executed - not once, in any browser, since .136.** The .136g field log: page loads ('die seite wird geladen, aber ich kann da immer noch nichts machen ... es steht auch nur main_ bei menuebaum ... beim klicken passiert nix'). Root cause chain (all three found by the NEW integration test T8, which parses and runs the REAL shipped page in node against the REAL component response): (1) **SYNTAX ERROR SINCE R201** - the JSON embed replaced only the /*EMBEDDED_MENU_JSON*/ marker and left the placeholder's trailing ' null;' in place: 'const EMBEDDED_MENU_JSON = {...} null;' does not parse, so the whole script block died silently in every browser - no menu render (breadcrumb stuck at the static '__main'), no click handlers, no fallback, nothing. Masked for 9 releases by the 403 layers on top (page unreadable, then POSTs rejected) - and by the test pyramid itself: every web test checked its own slice (evaluator functions vs jinja, JSON presence, HTTP status codes) - NOBODY EVER PARSED THE WHOLE SHIPPED SCRIPT. (2) Moonraker's envelope: every endpoint answer ships as {"result": ...} - the page read it raw, so even with a parsing script DOC.tree would have been undefined. (3) pollLive() was called synchronously at script end, BEFORE loadDoc()'s fetch resolved (LIVE still false) - live values would have NEVER polled. FIXES: embed hardened in BOTH branches (the splice branch eats the stray placeholder 'null'), the page unwraps the result envelope + shape-checks before use, pollLive starts after a successful loadDoc. T8 now runs the complete page script (DOM/fetch stubs, envelope included) against the real component response AND the embedded fallback - menu renders (13 items in the runner), badge goes 'Werte live', tree-down falls back to the embedded export. Also riding along from the same field log: klippy came up only after a manual FIRMWARE_RESTART once (MCU refused the automated serial reset 112x - a klipper service Restart=always loop while the MCU sulked; did NOT recur in the second log; if it ever recurs: full POWER OFF for 10 s, the .136a lesson) and the post-update window showed 50% python + 712 KB/s swap (boot-time scans on the Pi Zero - give it ~2 min after an update, per the manual). | **.136g (R208)** | **FIELD FIX - 'schon beim offnen der Seite' 403, auch inkognito: nginx could not READ the page.** The .136f field diagnosis (run BY the user, verbatim output): `sudo -u www-data head ... web/index.html` -> 'Permission denied'. nginx runs as www-data; a static file it cannot read is answered with 403 Forbidden - ALREADY ON OPENING THE PAGE, on every browser, cache or not, IP or hostname. The serve path ran through /home/pi - home-dir traverse permissions can flip on ANY update (Raspberry Pi OS tightens, an unpack lands oddly, whatever flipped here). The nasty part: the API proxy paths kept working (moonraker answered everything) - which is exactly why the R207 self-test, the E2E and every proxy check were green while the page would not even load: NOBODY tested the static page itself. FIXES: (1) the page is served from WEB_ROOT (/var/www/r2lcd) now - world-readable, OUTSIDE every home directory, the standard place for web roots; install_web.sh copies index.html there (sudo, 644/755) and --update-only re-syncs it on every package update (R2_WEB_ROOT override for tests). (2) the installer self-test GETs THE PAGE ITSELF now (must answer 200) in addition to the guard POSTs - an unreadable serve path can never pass silently again; on failure it names the exact check commands (ls -la, sudo -u www-data head). (3) --status reports the page location; --uninstall removes it. E2E with real nginx 1.26 AND a 750-mode home dir (www-data locked out, the old alias proven 403): page 200, live 200, same-origin command 200, foreign origin 403 at the proxy, refresh restores a stale page, self-test retry path exercised. Field-bug class #15: serve-path file permissions - static, outside every code test, visible only to a self-test that requests the actual page. Battery: r197 432/0 (+4 web tests: page at serve root, alias target, page refresh, page-in-self-test), r142 132/0, r81 23/23 (stamp-only re-freeze, proven), macro_render 1910/1910, r110 10/10, tier report 22/22. |
| **.136f (R207)** | **FIELD FIX - 'web ui ist wieder erreichbar ... aber jetzt kommt wieder 403' + a loaded gun defused.** The .136e field log: .136e confirmed installed (build stamp + single state pushes - the .136d twin watcher is GONE), host healthy (HOST_CHECK verdict OK) - but command POSTs 403 again. Root-cause analysis against Moonraker SOURCE (authorization.py, read this time instead of guessed): 403 = 'Unapproved Origin' from check_cors, 401 = untrusted IP, and the Host header is irrelevant for auth. Two ways my R206 fix could still fail in the field, both fixed: (1) A WRITTEN SITE FILE IS NOT A LIVE CONFIG - when the nginx reload silently fails, nginx keeps serving the OLD site (page loads, every command 403). install_web.sh now SELF-TESTS the live guard after every reload: a foreign-origin POST must be rejected BY NGINX (403, moonraker does not even need to be up), a same-origin POST must not be 403; on failure one reload retry (nginx -s reload), then the exact one-line fix is printed. The sandbox E2E exercised the retry path for real (first reload failed, retry passed). (2) CASE TRAP: the browser lowercases the Origin header but sends the Host exactly as typed - a bookmarked 'http://Ender-3.local:7126/' failed the case-sensitive origin==host map and answered 403. The map is case-insensitive (~*) now, and the proxied Host header is back to $host without port (field-proven original; auth never reads it). E2E with real nginx 1.26: full matrix green INCLUDING the mixed-case trap (200, was 403 with the old map), foreign origin 403 at the proxy, moonraker never sees an Origin. (3) RIDING ALONG - THE LOADED GUN: the duplicate-watcher matcher matched 'r2_postproc' ANYWHERE in a command line - a grep/nano/pgrep/bash that merely MENTIONS the name looked like a twin and the runtime sweep would have KILLED it (the new T6e test killed its own bash wrapper during development - in the field your 'grep r2_postproc ...' would have died). Now EXECUTION precision: a python interpreter with an r2_postproc*.py file among its ARGUMENTS. Plus: the sweep names the rival's full command line (from /proc) so the next doubled-push log says WHAT the second pusher was; update.sh runs a SECOND read-only watcher census at the very end (klippy up, after the boot races - the .136d doubling happened right AFTER a clean early census); the r142 bracket lint reads ast string-joins now (the .136d ROOT respond hid 'pkill -f ' + 'r2_postproc.py' across two lines - found and bracketed). Field-bug class #14: a written configuration is not the active configuration - verify behavior, not files. Battery: r197 430/0 (+T6d execution precision, +T6e rival-path sweep), r142 132/0 (ast lint + 2 fixtures), r81 23/23 (stamp-only re-freeze, proven), macro_render 1910/1910, r110 10/10, tier report 22/22. |
| **.136e (R206)** | **FIELD FIX - 'web ui sagt jetzt 403 forbidden': Moonraker's origin check killed every command - and the .136c fix had never reached the Pi.** Two stacked causes, both found in code and fixed. (1) THE 403: Moonraker answers 403 to every request whose Origin header it does not know - and browsers send Origin on EVERY command POST (same-origin GETs carry none). That is exactly why the web-LCD showed live values but 'kann da nix weiter machen' since .136b: GET /server/r2_menu/live passed (no Origin), every button's POST /printer/gcode/script died with 403 (the page shows 'Moonraker lehnte ab (403)'). FIX: the nginx site strips the Origin header at the proxy (the page is same-origin by construction - the header carries no information Moonraker could use) AND nginx takes over the browser-CSRF check itself: a map at the top of the site file lets only requests pass whose Origin is empty (GETs, curl, tools) or exactly this site (http://<host> == Host header) - a foreign website's Origin is answered 403 BEFORE it reaches the printer, at least as strict as the check it replaces. (2) THE DEPLOYMENT GAP: update.sh never synced web/r2_menu.py into moonraker's components/ - the .136b installer ran once, so the R204 slim query NEVER REACHED THE FIELD (the 403 report made that visible). install_web.sh gains --update-only: update.sh refreshes component + nginx site on EVERY package update now (idempotent, nginx -t gated, clean NO-OP when no web-LCD is installed - the web-LCD stays opt-in). Riding along: the installer writes the nginx site via sudo when /etc/nginx is not user-writable (site chmod 644, read fallbacks for status/port detection - both found by the live E2E), the proxied Host header keeps its port ($http_host), and --status reports whether the CSRF guard is present. VERIFIED END TO END with real nginx 1.26 + a fake moonraker: page 200, live GET proxied without Origin, the same-origin command POST proxied WITHOUT Origin (the 403 case - now 200), a foreign-origin POST answered 403 BY NGINX (never reaches moonraker - proven by the hit log), --update-only restores a stale component and keeps the port. Field-bug class #13: the auth layer between proxy and API (+ code in the zip is not code on the Pi - deploy what you ship). Battery: r197 428/0 (+4 web-LCD tests), r142 130/0, r81 23/23 (stamp-only re-freeze, proven), macro_render 1910/1910, r110 10/10, tier report 22/22. |
| **.136d (R205)** | **FIELD FEEDBACK - 'sudo zeigt uns die Wahrheit' - a measurement artifact, now impossible to misread.** The .136c field log: update ran clean (build R2-2026-10-29.136c confirmed, daemon on new code) - then the user checked the watcher by hand: the check showed ONE process without sudo and THREE lines with sudo - read as hidden watchers that only root can see. TRUTH: the two extra lines WERE the 'sudo pgrep' command itself. pgrep -f matches the FULL command line; pgrep excludes only its own PID, not the sudo wrapper pair that carries the same pattern in its command line; /proc is world-readable, so plain pgrep hides nothing here. Real watcher count: exactly 1 (PID 2125) - and a second .136+ watcher cannot even start (kernel flock, R200 - it refuses loudly). MY bug: the daemon's flock-refusal hint and the .101-era verify line both told the user to run the bare pattern - the documentation manufactured this trap. Fixes: (1) EVERY shipped pgrep/pkill on the watcher uses the [r] bracket form now ('pgrep -af [r]2_postproc.py' - the regex cannot match the checker's own command line, with or without sudo); (2) update.sh's WATCHER CENSUS now SAYS the number + PID after every update ('[ok] watcher census: exactly 1 r2-postproc watcher (PID 2125) - started with this update, running the new code') instead of a silent ok - silence is what sent the user to pgrep; census >1 prints ONE fix command (sudo bash update.sh), census 0 keeps the start hint; (3) the root pkill in update.sh is bracket-protected too - an unbracketed root pkill matches its OWN sudo wrapper and could signal its parent mid-run; same fix in the daemon's root-relic takeover hint; (4) r197 T6a asserts the bracket form in the refusal hint; (5) NEW r142 lint: no unbracketed pgrep/pkill watcher pattern may ship on any live surface (scripts, daemon, manual, test docs - the README changelog is exempt, history may quote old commands). MANUAL troubleshooting row added, DISPLAYS_WEB_UI.pdf field-bug classes completed to (12) ((10) watcher boot doubling + silent klippy-down, (11) live-endpoint load). Field-bug class #12: measurement artifacts - the diagnostic shows up in its own measurement. Battery: r197 424/0, r142 130/0 (new lint), r81 23/23 (stamp-only re-freeze, PROVEN: the new module run with the old stamp string reproduces all 23 .136c goldens byte-identical), r110 all 10 scenarios, macro_render 1910/1910, tier report 22/22. |
| **.136c (R204)** | **FIELD FIX - the web-LCD live poll blocked klippy 0.7 s ('Neopixel update did not succeed').** The field klippy.log showed it plainly: every page poll made klippy's reactor 'busy for 0.699/0.761 s' inside QueryStatusHelper._do_query, and right after it a 'Neopixel update did not succeed' - the LED missed its schedule because klippy was busy serializing my query. Root cause: the /server/r2_menu/live endpoint queried EVERY gcode_macro object (~300 on the field Pi, each serializing all its variables) plus save_variables - 0.7 s of reactor-blocking work on the Pi Zero 2 W, every 3 s while the page was open. Exactly the host-overload class that caused the .125 'Timer too close' shutdowns, now self-inflicted via websocket. FIX: the exporter extracts live_objects - the EXACT set of printer objects the menu name:/enable: templates reference (12 on the field Pi: 4 gcode_macro objects + save_variables, print_stats, gcode_move, bed_mesh, configfile, exclude_object, input_shaper, ...) - into menu_structure.json; the live endpoint queries ONLY that set (intersected with the real object list, plus a small DEFAULT fallback for old exports), prunes fat values as before, and caches 2.5 s so a 3 s page poll triggers at most ONE real klippy query, shared across tabs. Self-maintaining: a new menu object reference lands in the export automatically. Regression tests (r197, 424 checks now): the fake klippy serves an object list with 300 JUNK macros + records every query's want-set - the live endpoint must request <=20 objects, none of the junk, but _STATE + save_variables; the export must carry live_objects (with _STATE/save_variables, without generator macros); and the evaluator fixture may only contain objects /live really ships. Field-bug class #11: websocket status queries are host load - cheap by construction, not by measurement afterwards. Battery: r197 424/0, r81 23/23 (stamp-only re-freeze, diff-verified). |
| **.136b (R203)** | **WEB-LCD IN ONE COMMAND (field feedback: the manual 3-step path was too fiddly).** New web/install_web.sh does the whole setup: copies the Moonraker extension into components/, appends a MARKED [r2_menu] section to moonraker.conf (backup first, idempotent), and drops its OWN nginx site file (sites-available/r2lcd + symlink) that serves web/index.html on the first free port of 7126/7186/7196 and proxies /server/ + /printer/ to moonraker - the Mainsail/Fluidd nginx config is NEVER touched, nginx -t gates the reload (rollback on error), and --uninstall removes every trace again (--status shows what is installed). The user runs ONE command and opens ONE printed URL (http://<pi>:7126/r2lcd/) - same-origin via the proxy, so the live values work; bookmark on the phone and done. Why not moonraker-served HTML: register_static_file_handler is documented to force Content-Disposition: attachment (a download, not a page), so the page needs a real webserver - nginx is already there on MainsailOS. | **.136a (R202)** | **FIELD HOTFIX - 'a twin watcher still spawns at boot' + klippy-down visibility.** The .136 clean-restart field log showed: (1) the update arrived and klipper healthy ('Macro system OK ... .136'), (2) the FIRMWARE_RESTART failure after the update was healed by the full Pi restart (an MCU a soft reset cannot reconfigure - only a power cycle does; documented now), (3) at boot, pushes still doubled for the first seconds (sd_tree/gen_files twice in the SAME second = two watchers started together), then went single - the .136 first-cycle sweep killed the twin, but SOMETHING still spawns one at every boot. | **.134 (R188/R189)** | **FIELD ROUND: FEEDBACK HYGIENE + PRESET SAVE + TEXTURE SOURCE + PAUSE HYGIENE.** Three field bugs root-caused and fixed, plus the .134 wish list coded: (1) PRESET SAVE DID NOTHING (.133 field report): Klipper parses extended commands with POSIX shlex - a NAKED dict literal loses every single quote, literal_eval fails and the error surfaced only in the console; SAVE/DELETE now wrap the dict in double quotes (same rule as _sv_line/_opt_compact), and a new R188 roundtrip test pushes every rendered SAVE_VARIABLE through the REAL parser chain (shlex posix + literal_eval). (2) PAUSE RIGHT AFTER THE PURGE LINE: planned pauses (pause_next_layer/pause_at_layer) survived the end of their print and fired at the FIRST LAYER_MARK of the next one - PRINT_START clears them now (with a RESPOND note when something was actually cleared). (3) THE TEXTURE TOOL NEVER BUILT A FILE (macro level, R189): jinja's default() only fires on UNDEFINED, so an EMPTY _PREVIEW.name (push skipped while busy / not arrived yet) never fell through to preview_file - and the preview push carries the BARE basename, so files from subfolders were 'not found'. preview_file (set synchronously at selection, WITH its subfolder path) is the primary source now, _PREVIEW.name only a fallback; SCALE already read preview_file directly (chain audited: macro -> watcher -> apply_scale -> auto-select, no bug found - the 'does not print' symptom is the no-autostart UX path, covered by the new feedback). (4) MENU FEEDBACK HYGIENE: 92 frozen enable conditions removed from browser/generator/optimized slots (Klipper freezes the enable list at menu entry - rows could stay dead dots until the next visit; names render live ~0.5s), Optimized is 40 live slots in the main menu AND pre-print (was a one-file picker), and 48 menu.exit() kicks make every confirmed generator/fix row LEAVE the menu automatically (second press = start + out). (5) ONE unified R2-D2 melody set project-wide, silent while printing: r2_start = work begins (every create/fix press), r2_gen = queued-job build, r2_done = success, r2_confirm = quick confirmations; quiet_print now also covers start/gen/done - during a print the LED carries the feedback. (6) FIXES ARE LIVE WORK: gen_busy guards in PRINT_AS_SLICED/AS_LEGACY/PRINT_WITH_OVERRIDES/SDCARD_PRINT_FILE plus watcher pushes (file_fix/worker 1-before-try/0-in-finally) - a print start WAITS while a fix rewrites the file (LCD: 'Wait: generating'), the queue notice shrank from 90s to 10s. (user request - replaces the R62/R109 auto-capture):** after a successful print that was live-tuned, ONE question appears on the LCD: YES saves the print's tuning as an optimized copy, NO (or 90 s of silence) restores the exact pre-print baseline - nothing ever flows into Pre-Print or a profile silently anymore. (2) **OPTIMIZED COPIES (non-invasive, md5-proof):** YES writes a copy to gcodes/optimized/<same name> via the watcher - the ORIGINAL is only ever read (diff/md5 proof). The copy carries the tuned values BAKEN in: percents (flow, per-feature speed, global M220 speed) are scaled into the file's absolute values AND neutralized in the replay set (never applied twice), absolute values (PA, accel, first-layer speed, fan limits) bake for the display and replay idempotently; everything without a display key (temps, retraction, per-feature flow/PA) rides in the _OPT_BAKED replay line. Header tags: '; R2-OPT' source+date and the baked list. Re-optimizing overwrites (newest round wins); same basename from another subfolder gets a prefixed name. (3) **PRINTING THE COPY:** Klipper's stock LCD file list is ROOT-ONLY (vsdlist never reads subfolders - verified in menu.py), so the new top-level 'Optimized' menu lists the folder (pick number, START - the start dialog guards as always - or Values > Pre-Print); Mainsail shows the folder natively. Selecting the copy shows 'OPTIMIZED - tuned values baked' in File info instead of raw slicer values. (4) **E2 - FROM OPTIMIZED (user request):** Pre-Print > Profiles > From optimized picks any copy and loads its values as the Pre-Print STARTING POINT for the next print (nothing written to any file); 'Save last print > optimized' is the second chance if the question was missed. (5) The auto-'post' profile and its Auto-save toggle are RETIRED (old post entries stay loadable/deletable); YOUR manual pre profiles + auto-load are untouched. Unexpected end of template, looking for 'endif'` - the new OUT= dispatch lines in GEN_BRICKKIT/GEN_FIGURES sat at COLUMN 0 inside the gcode body. Klipper does not read bodies with our regexes: configfile.py strips every line at the first `#`, then `configparser.RawConfigParser(strict=False, inline_comment_prefixes=(';', '#'))` parses the file - and an UNINDENTED line inside a value is a NEW OPTION (`name=value`), so the `gcode:` value was silently truncated inside the open `{% if %}` block. Fixed (indentation); GEN_FIGURES carried the same landmine one macro later. New **tools/r166_template_check.py** replays klipper's EXACT pipeline over every macro template in the repo: per-line #-strip, the configparser call, `jinja2.Environment('{%', '%}', '{', '}')` compile of every gcode value, an option allowlist per section type (a column-0 line shows up as a bogus option), and a diff between what our regexes see and what configparser returns. It reproduces the .124 error word for word on the broken file and passes on the fixed one (21 cfg files, 2062 sections, 337 templates) - the whole battery stayed green on .124 because the render test extracts bodies with regexes that tolerate column-0 lines; this class of bug can never ship again. (2) **Printing LED motion back (user request):** the .123 static blue was the emergency fix; the real budget analysis says a slow 4 s breathing at 5 fps costs 5 serial msg/s = ~1% of the 450 msg/s budget - the .122 death queue (75/100 LED messages) came from 24 fps plus readback resends; a 3 s host stall now queues 15 of 100 messages instead of 72. r164's LED guard enforces `frame_rate <= 5` + static/breathing layer for r2_printing. | **.114 (R153)** | **Geometry tagging perfected: the vase rule, the brim rule, and the answer to 'does fuzzy skin work now?'.** The R111 geometry classifier (loops->walls by nesting, parallel lines->infill, layer 1->bottom, last->top - it already tagged UNTAGGED files with derived APPLY_TYPE lines at confidence 0.7) learns the field rules: (1) **Vase rule** - a run of extruding moves that carry a slowly rising Z is a spiral; after the bottom ONE feature remains and that is OUTER WALL, always (no more fragment 'layers' from the 0.05mm Z micro-steps). (2) **Brim rule** - a layer-1 wall loop with NO successor on layer 2 is a BRIM (walls continue upward, brim does not); the real outer wall, nested inside the brim ring, is promoted back to WALL-OUTER. (3) **Bug fix**: derived APPLY_TYPE lines were keyed by the RAW line index while the writer counts filtered lines - they landed next to the wrong lines or nowhere. End-to-end proof with an untagged vase file: brim ring -> BRIM, real wall -> WALL-OUTER, 12 bottom lines -> SURFACE-BOTTOM, all 1428 spiral moves -> WALL-OUTER, and the fuzzy texture on that processed file shifts exactly 1432 vertices (the whole spiral + the outer wall, brim untouched) - fuzzy skin now works for tagged AND untagged vase prints. This is the foundation the user sketched: geometry understanding for perfect tagging (also the basis for object localization / plotting onto printed objects in Z - next steps). NOT a concurrency/rate problem: Klipper serializes all gcode in one mutex, a single malformed push suffices - micro-pauses would have changed nothing. Verify after updating: update.sh ends with the watcher census ('exactly 1 r2-postproc watcher (PID ...)'); by hand use the self-match-proof `pgrep -af '[r]2_postproc.py'` (exactly ONE process - under sudo a bare pattern lists the sudo+pgrep pair itself, .136d), then FIRMWARE_RESTART. (3) Layer-range excerpts keep the source's EXCLUDE_OBJECT_DEFINE blocks, so KAMP meshes adaptively instead of falling back to a full-bed mesh. |
| **.53 (R69)** | **HOTFIX**: `SAVE_VARIABLE` could SHUT DOWN the printer — Klipper strips the quotes (POSIX shlex) and only catches ValueError, so any string with a space, an apostrophe, an empty value or the label queue's pipes raised SyntaxError = "Internal error on command" = shutdown. All R63/R64 string saves (label text/queue, plot-name editor, reso/shaper/plot-file stores, 4 watcher pushes) moved to the escaped quote form + quote/backslash validation + an `_sv_line` builder in the watcher. New test gate replays Klipper's exact parser over every cfg line and sweeps the whole charset through the label editor. Also removed `;` from the charset (Klipper cuts every command line at the first `;` — it could never be saved). |
| **.52 (R68)** | **HOTFIX**: the watcher crashed on 5 foreign files with `ValueError: could not convert string to float: '-'` — the G0/G1 parameter regex accepted digit-less tokens, so hyphenated names in trailing comments (`; Felge-V-035`) or bare `E-` tokens killed the whole processing run (files stayed untagged forever). Fixed: comments stripped before parsing, regex requires a digit, per-token guard; phantom params from comment text (`; Ender-3` → `R=-3`) gone too. Affected files are picked up automatically on the next service start. |
| **.51 (R67)** | **HOTFIX**: 2nd boot blocker from .47 — the label job-queue menu name used the non-existent Jinja filter `\|string.split` → "Internal error during connect" right after the `#` fix surfaced. Fixed with the proven `.split()` pattern; new test gate compiles ALL ~1565 boot-compiled templates (macro gcode + menu name/enable/gcode) and renders every menu template — this error class can never ship again. |
| **.50 (R66)** | **Update via browser, one command**: upload the ZIP in Mainsail's config folder (where printer.cfg lives, next to `klipper-e3-r2d2`) and run `bash ~/printer_data/config/klipper-e3-r2d2/update.sh` — it unpacks the newest uploaded ZIP over the old folder, checks everything and restarts (python3 fallback if `unzip` is missing, downgrade guard). |
| **.49 (R65)** | **HOTFIX**: `#` in `variable_charset` halts Klipper at boot (config comment strip) — charset fixed, `#` still pickable on the LCD (last entry), validator now emulates Klipper's strip + literal_eval and scans all cfg lines for `#` inside quotes. |
| **.48 (R64)** | **Resonance diagram**: the shaper CSVs become a printed (or pen-plotted) plate — PSD curve, all 5 shaper curves, shaper table with the winner boxed; pick it in `Vibration Cal. > Apply result` (frequency from the table, live + boot override, faithful port of Klipper's fit math). **Bridging test**: two towers diagonally + a short span (10–50 %) clearly below on its own lower tower, everything live-tunable. **Plotter**: text files (`PLOT_TEXT`) + status report (`PLOT_STATUS`) on one auto-fitted page, dark writing boards auto-inverted (border heuristic), start overrides + live plotter menu. **Simplified update** (`update.sh`, no new includes since .47). |
| **.47 (R63)** | Label generator (golden-ratio plates, 3 fonts, holes, 2 colors, purge tower), guarded Save config, config review. |
